([nadyshalaby/hackify](https://github.com/nadyshalaby/hackify))
- **hackify-edge**: Bleeding-edge hackify, tracks the `main` branch directly. SUPPLY-CHAIN NOTE: unreleased commits (including any new executable code under `hooks/` and `scripts/`) land in your plugin tree on every plugin refresh, with no tag pinning or signature verification. A force-push or compromised maintainer commit reaches you the next time Claude Code refreshes the marketplace. Install ONLY if you accept that risk and read every diff. Production users MUST pin to the `hackify` channel (latest signed tag) instead. ([nadyshalaby/hackify](https://github.com/nadyshalaby/hackify))

- **hammer-worlds**: Mounts the hosted coverage-intelligence world over HTTP and carries the one skill that is about the discipline rather than any corpus: how to route a question through the worlds a client has mounted, why two worlds' answers never compose into a third claim, how to read a refusal, and the claim report every answer built on a world has to close with. Each world ships its own routing instructions from its own deployed build, so nothing here can describe a version that no longer exists. ([hmmrlabs/hammer-plugin](https://github.com/hmmrlabs/hammer-plugin))
- **hammerspoon**: Automate macOS with Hammerspoon Lua scripting. Window management, hotkeys, Spoons, and CLI integration via hs command. ([plinde/claude-plugins](https://github.com/plinde/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/hammerspoon))
- **hammock**: Hammock Driven Development as a coding agent skill. Think before you code. ([adrianleb/hammock-skill](https://github.com/adrianleb/hammock-skill))
- **hamsamilton-lang-tutor**: Gives grammar corrections and vocabulary help in your target language. ([hamsamilton/lang-tutor](https://github.com/hamsamilton/lang-tutor))
- **hamster**: Orchestrates Hamster Studio brief execution with team personas, parallel wave execution, and deep review modes ([gethamster/plugin](https://github.com/gethamster/plugin))
- **hamster**: Win rewards while your agent codes. A QR card for a live rewarded game appears on every prompt — scan it with your phone (any OS), install the game, and earn credits as you hit in-game goals. /wallet shows your earnings. ([growlads/hamster-plugin](https://github.com/growlads/hamster-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/hamster))
- **hamsurang-slide**: Hamsurang brand HTML presentation generator. Soft Modern design with light/dark themes, 14 slide types, 4-color code highlighting. ([hamsurang/kit](https://github.com/hamsurang/kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/hamsurang-slide))
- **han**: Meta-plugin that installs the bundled Han suite. Has no components of its own; it depends on han-communication, han-core, han-documentation, han-research, han-planning, han-coding, han-github, and han-reporting so installing it pulls them all in. Does not bundle the opt-in han-feedback, han-atlassian, or han-plugin-builder plugins. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han))
- **han-atlassian**: Atlassian-facing extensions to the Han suite. Adds markdown-to-confluence, which publishes a local Markdown file to a user-specified Confluence page; project-documentation-to-confluence, which runs the han-documentation project-documentation skill and then publishes the result there; investigate-to-confluence, which runs the core investigate skill and publishes the resulting investigation report there; code-overview-to-confluence, which runs the core code-overview skill and publishes the resulting overview there; plan-a-feature-to-confluence, which runs the core plan-a-feature skill and publi… ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-atlassian))
- **han-coding**: Code-writing and execution skills for the Han suite. Home of the tdd skill, which drives a feature or behavior through a BDD-framed red-green-refactor loop with an enforced observed-failure gate. Depends on han-core and han-communication; bundled by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-coding))
- **han-communication**: Foundational communication plugin for the Han suite. Owns the canonical readability standard, writing-voice profile, and explanation standard, the readability-guidance skill that surfaces the first two into a calling skill's context for in-voice drafting, the explanation-guidance skill that surfaces the third at the point a run talks to a person, the readability-editor agent that runs the adversarial rewrite, and the edit-for-readability skill. Depends on nothing and sits beneath every other plugin; the plugins that produce prose output depend on it. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-communication))
- **han-core**: The shared foundation of the Han suite: the specialist agent roster the other plugins dispatch, the project-discovery skill with its project-scanner agent, and the canonical evidence and YAGNI rule files. The documentation skills live in han-documentation, the pre-planning research skills in han-research, the planning skills in han-planning, and the coding skills in han-coding; each depends on han-core. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-core))
- **han-ddd**: Strategic Domain-Driven Design analysis for the Han suite. Discovers domain boundaries, identifies bounded context candidates (strongly expressed, latent, and speculative), finds boundary leaks, and produces an evidence-backed domain and context map for an existing codebase. Depends on han-communication and han-core. Opt-in: not bundled by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-ddd))
- **han-documentation**: Documentation skills for the Han suite: writing down what the team built and decided. Home of project-documentation, architectural-decision-record, and runbook. Depends on han-communication and han-core; bundled by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-documentation))
- **han-feedback**: Post-session feedback skill for the Han suite. Captures structured observations, ratings, and improvement ideas on the Han skills you ran in a session and optionally posts them as a GitHub issue to testdouble/han. Opt-in: installed on its own, not pulled in by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-feedback))
- **han-github**: GitHub-specific extensions to Han. Provides skills to post code reviews as PR comments, update a PR description with a consistent format, and other GitHub-facing workflows. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-github))
- **han-linear**: Linear-facing extensions to the Han suite. Adds work-items-to-linear, which creates one Linear issue per slice from a work-items file. Works through the Linear MCP server and requires it to be configured. Depends on no other Han plugin. Opt-in: installed on its own, not pulled in by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-linear))
- **han-planning**: Planning skills for the Han suite: specifying, planning, sequencing, breaking down, and stress-testing work before implementation. Home of plan-a-feature, plan-implementation, plan-a-phased-build, plan-work-items, and iterative-plan-review, plus the discussion-facilitator agent. Depends on han-communication and han-core; bundled by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-planning))
- **han-plugin-builder**: Guidance for building Claude Code skills, agents, and plugins. The guidance skill answers authoring questions and, run with init, vendors the full guidance set into a repo as a path-scoped rule index so the right guidance surfaces while editing skill and agent files. Opt-in and dependency-free: installed on its own, not pulled in by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-plugin-builder))
- **han-plugins**: Validates Han plugin structure and configuration files. ([TheBushidoCollective/han](https://github.com/TheBushidoCollective/han/tree/HEAD/plugins/specialized/han-plugins))
- **han-reporting**: Reporting and summary skills for the Han suite. Turns feature specifications into plain-language stakeholder summaries (also called executive or business summaries) with diagrams, for sharing with non-technical stakeholders before implementation kicks off. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-reporting))
- **han-research**: Pre-planning knowledge-work skills for the Han suite: understanding a problem before anyone commits to a plan. Home of research, gap-analysis, and issue-triage, plus the research-analyst agent. Depends on han-communication and han-core; bundled by the han meta-plugin. ([testdouble/han](https://github.com/testdouble/han/tree/HEAD/han-research))

- **hone**: Test-driven, largely unattended development for Claude Code. You write a short plan for one change, and an automated loop builds it test-first in an isolated git worktree, runs every check, reviews the result, and merges it. Two characteristics define it. The honing: docs stay minimal, hone deletes anything the code, types, or tests already show, and a garden pass between changes keeps cutting what goes stale. The enforcement: hooks allow no production code without a failing test, block red checks, and hold irreversible or real-environment changes at land gates for a sign-off. ([dominik-rehse/hone](https://github.com/dominik-rehse/hone))
